| [Treas. Reg. § 1.6011-10(g)(1)](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-26/section-1.6011-10#p-1.6011-10(g)(1)) | Treas. Reg. § 1.6011-10(g)(1). A participant in a micro-captive listed transaction disclosing it on Form 8886 must describe the transaction in sufficient detail, including when, how, and from whom it became aware of the transaction and how it participated in it. | For all participants, describing the transaction in sufficient detail includes, but is not limited to, describing on Form 8886 (or successor form) when, how, and from whom the participant became aware of the transaction, and how the participant participated in the transaction (for example, as an Insured, a Captive, or other participant). |  |
| [Treas. Reg. § 31.6205-1(a)(4)](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-26/section-31.6205-1#p-31.6205-1(a)(4)) | Treas. Reg. § 31.6205-1(a)(4). An employer correcting an underpayment of employment taxes on an adjusted return (such as Form 941-X) designates the period in which the error was ascertained and the period corrected and explains in detail the grounds and facts relied on to support the correction. | (4) Every adjusted return on which an underpayment is corrected pursuant to this section shall designate the return period in which the error was ascertained and the return period being corrected, explain in detail the grounds and facts relied upon to support the correction, and set forth such other information as may be required by the regulations in this section and by the instructions relating to the adjusted return. |  |
| [Treas. Reg. § 31.6402(a)-2(a)(1)(ii)](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-26/section-31.6402(a)-2#p-31.6402(a)-2(a)(1)(ii)) | Treas. Reg. § 31.6402(a)-2(a)(1)(ii) (claims for credit or refund of FICA or RRTA tax). The claim, filed on the IRS-prescribed form, must designate the return period and explain in detail the grounds and facts relied on to support the claim. | The claim for credit or refund must be filed on the form prescribed by the IRS and must designate the return period to which the claim relates, explain in detail the grounds and facts relied upon to support the claim, and set forth such other information as may be required by this section and by the instructions relating to the form used to make such claim. |  |
| [Treas. Reg. § 31.6413(a)-2(a)(3)](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-26/section-31.6413(a)-2#p-31.6413(a)-2(a)(3)) | Treas. Reg. § 31.6413(a)-2(a)(3). An employer correcting an overpayment of employment taxes on an adjusted return (such as Form 941-X) designates the period in which the error was ascertained and the period corrected and explains in detail the grounds and facts relied on to support the correction. | Every adjusted return shall designate the return period in which the error was ascertained and the return period being corrected, explain in detail the grounds and facts relied upon to support the correction, and set forth such other information as may be required by this section and § 31.6413(a)-1 and by the instructions relating to the adjusted return. |  |
| [Treas. Reg. § 301.6707A-1(e)(3)(i)](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-26/section-301.6707A-1#p-301.6707A-1(e)(3)(i)) | Treas. Reg. § 301.6707A-1(e)(3)(i) (rescission of the penalty for failing to disclose a reportable transaction). For an untimely Form 8886 to weigh in favor of rescission, the taxpayer files an amended return, reflecting no other changes, with the Form 8886 attached, and states the reason for filing the amended return in the explanation-of-changes space. | The amended return filed with the untimely Form 8886 (or successor form) must not reflect any other changes to the return that it amends, and the taxpayer must, in the space provided for an explanation of changes on the amended return, state the reason for filing the amended return. |  |
| [Treas. Reg. § 301.7811-1(b)](https://www.ecfr.gov/current/title-26/section-301.7811-1#p-301.7811-1(b)) | Treas. Reg. § 301.7811-1(b) (taxpayer assistance orders). A taxpayer seeking a TAO files Form 911 or a written statement giving the Taxpayer Advocate Service enough information to determine the nature of the harm or the need for assistance. | A request for a TAO shall be made on a Form 911, “Request for Taxpayer Advocate Service Assistance (And Application for Taxpayer Assistance Order)” (or other specified form) or in a written statement that provides sufficient information for the Taxpayer Advocate Service (TAS) to determine the nature of the harm or the need for assistance. |  |

| [Instructions for Form 990, Schedule H, at 3](https://www.irs.gov/pub/irs-pdf/i990sh.pdf) | Instructions for Schedule H (Form 990), Part I, line 7, column (d). A hospital organization may describe in Part VI any inconsistencies between its current reporting of direct offsetting revenue and its reporting in prior years. | Organizations may describe any inconsistencies from reporting in prior years in Part VI. |  |
| [Instructions for Form 990, Schedule H, at 6](https://www.irs.gov/pub/irs-pdf/i990sh.pdf) | Instructions for Schedule H (Form 990), Part III, Section B, line 7. A hospital organization may describe in Part VI Medicare revenues and costs not included in its Medicare Cost Reports, such as those for ambulatory surgery centers, physician services, and Medicare Part C and D programs. | The organization can describe in Part VI the Medicare revenues and costs not included in its Medicare Cost Report(s) for the year (for example, revenues and costs for freestanding ambulatory surgery centers, physician services billed by the organization, clinical laboratory services, and revenues and costs of Medicare Part C and Part D programs). |  |
| [Instructions for Form 990, Schedule H, at 17](https://www.irs.gov/pub/irs-pdf/i990sh.pdf) | Instructions for Schedule H (Form 990), Worksheet 3 (Medicaid), line 6. A hospital organization may describe in Part VI the amount of prior-year Medicaid revenue included in its reported Medicaid net patient service revenue. | Organizations can describe in Part VI the amount of prior year Medicaid revenue included in Part I, line 7b. |  |
| [Instructions for Form 990, Schedule H, at 21](https://www.irs.gov/pub/irs-pdf/i990sh.pdf) | Instructions for Schedule H (Form 990), Worksheet 7 (research), Part I, line 7h. Although research funded by non-exempt, non-governmental sources cannot be counted as community benefit, the hospital organization may describe such research in Part VI, including its cost, funder, and how its results are made available to the public. | However, the organization can describe in Part VI any research it conducts that isn't funded by tax-exempt or government entities, including the cost of such research; the identity of the funder; how the results of such research are made available to the public, if at all; and whether the results are made available to the public at no cost or nominal cost. |  |
| [Instructions for Form 990-T, at 34](https://www.irs.gov/pub/irs-pdf/i990t.pdf) | Instructions for Schedule A (Form 990-T), Part XI (Supplemental Information). The organization uses Part XI to explain its operations and support amounts reported, and may enter an explanation of any change in the business activity code from a prior year. | Part XI. Supplemental Information Use Schedule A (Form 990-T), Part XI to: • Explain the organization’s operations, • Provide information for lines that don’t have an embedded attachment to capture the information to supplement information provided on an embedded attachment, or • Provide any other information in support of amounts reported on Schedule A (Form 990-T). An organization that associated unrelated trade or business activity with a different NAICS or Business Activity Code in a prior year than the NAICS or Business Activity Code shown on the Schedule A (Form 990-T) for the current tax year can enter the explanation for the change here. |  |
| [Instructions for Form 5330, at 12](https://www.irs.gov/pub/irs-pdf/i5330.pdf) | Form 5330 instructions, prohibited transactions, listing of other participating disqualified persons. If more space is needed, the filer may attach a statement fully explaining the correction and identifying the persons involved in the prohibited transaction. | If additional space is needed, you may attach a statement fully explaining the correction and identifying persons involved in the prohibited transaction. |  |
| [Instructions for Form 8975, at 6](https://www.irs.gov/pub/irs-pdf/i8975.pdf) | Instructions for Schedule A (Form 8975), Part II. A paper filer limited to three main business activity codes that feels they do not properly reflect an entity’s main businesses may enter additional codes and explain in Part III. | However, if you feel this does not properly reflect the main businesses of a constituent entity, you may use Part III, Additional Information, on the appropriate Schedule A (Form 8975) to enter additional codes and explain. |  |
| [Instructions for Form 8975, at 6](https://www.irs.gov/pub/irs-pdf/i8975.pdf) | Instructions for Schedule A (Form 8975), Part III. The filer may enter any information or explanation it deems necessary to facilitate understanding of the jurisdiction and constituent entity information, including the financial and employee data in Part I. | Additionally, you can enter any relevant information or explanation that you deem necessary or that would facilitate the understanding of the information provided in Parts I and II. The information may or may not relate to a specific constituent entity. The information may be used to explain the tax jurisdiction financial and employee information in Part I. |  |
| [Publication 5, at 6](https://www.irs.gov/pub/irs-pdf/p5.pdf) | Publication 5, Innocent Spouse Appeals. A requesting or non-requesting spouse appealing an innocent spouse determination must request the appeal in writing and may use Form 12509 to explain why they disagree with the IRS’s proposed determination. | Your appeal request must be submitted in writing. You can use Form 12509, Innocent Spouse Statement of Disagreement, to explain why you disagree with the IRS’s proposed determination concerning your request or your (current or former) spouse’s request for innocent spouse relief. |  |
